# NxTerra structure registry inventory

Static inventory from the archived main server build, Minecraft 1.21.1 vanilla data and the matching published Structurify configuration. Spacing and separation are in chunks. Placement estimates require a registry dump or seed runs to become measured baselines.

Server mod JARs scanned: **226**. Active-root/matched built-in structure-set IDs: **299**. All structure-set IDs: **300**. Structure-definition rows: **639**. Parse failures: **1**.

Snapshot enabled datapacks: amendments:generated_server_pack, builtin/compat_recipes/aether_lost_content_not_compat, create:dynamic_data, create_dragons_plus:runtime, eclipticseasons/Rain Together, eclipticseasons/Regional Snow Time, eclipticseasons/Vanilla Biome Climate Settings, generated/fragmentum_layer, mod/explorify, mod/geophilic, mod/hearths, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_badlands, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_birch_forest, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_dark_forest, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_desert, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_flower_forest, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_jungle, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_mountain_biomes, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_plains, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_savannas, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_swamp, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_taiga, mod/natures_spirit:resourcepacks/modified_windswept_hills, mod/yungsapi:locate_replacer, mod_data, spelunkery:generated_pack, supplementaries:generated_pack, t_and_t:resources/t_and_t_waystones_patch, vanilla.

Snapshot disabled datapacks: builtin/aether_ruined_portal, builtin/aether_temporary_freezing, bundle, ecologics:no_maple_trees, mod/fishofthieves:simple_spawning_condition_pack, mod/lootr:datapacks/lootr_no_advancements, mod/lootr:datapacks/lootr_no_suspicious_blocks, trade_rebalance.

Sources overriding vanilla Overworld noise settings: Minecraft 1.21.1, lithosphere-1.9.jar, still-life-0.1.1.jar.

Global Structurify spacing multiplier: **0.8**. Overlap prevention is enabled. The six configured per-structure flatness checks appear below the set inventory.
